# Root App Developer Documentation — API Reference

> TypeScript API reference for the Root App SDK (server and client). For conceptual guides, see llms-app-guides.txt.
> Generated: 2026-03-17 | Content-Hash: e40d6c85a94f

## Instructions

- Apps use `@rootsdk/server-app` (server) and `@rootsdk/client` (client). Bots use `@rootsdk/server-bot`.
- TypeScript-first. All examples use TypeScript.
- Server code runs in the Root cloud. Client code runs in a Chromium container.
- For real-time data, use event subscriptions, not polling.
- All async methods return Promises. Use async/await.
- Bots are server-only — same community API as apps, but no client UI.

> **Size:** ~163K tokens

## Table of Contents

### App API Reference

- [Client API Reference](#file-app-api-reference-client-index)
- [Server API Reference](#file-app-api-reference-server-index)

#### Access Rules

- [AccessRule](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-accessrule) - Represents a permission override for a specific role or member on a specific channel or channel group.
- [AccessRuleClient](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-accessruleclient) - Service client for managing access rules that control permissions for specific roles or members on channels and channel groups.
- [AccessRuleCreateRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-accessrulecreaterequest) - Request object for creating a new access rule that overrides permissions for a role or member on a channel or channel group.
- [AccessRuleCreateRoleOrMemberRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-accessrulecreateroleormemberrequest) - Request object for creating an access rule for a specific role or member.
- [AccessRuleDeleteRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-accessruledeleterequest) - Request object for deleting an access rule.
- [AccessRuleEditRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-accessruleeditrequest) - Request object for modifying an existing access rule's permission overlay.
- [AccessRuleGetRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-accessrulegetrequest) - Request object for retrieving a single access rule.
- [AccessRuleListByChannelOrChannelGroupRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-accessrulelistbychannelorchannelgrouprequest) - Request object for listing all access rules that apply to a specific channel or channel group.
- [AccessRuleListByRoleOrMemberRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-accessrulelistbyroleormemberrequest) - Request object for listing all access rules that apply to a specific role or member.
- [AccessRuleUpdateRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-accessruleupdaterequest) - Request object for batch creating, editing, and deleting access rules in a single operation.

- [AppGuid (client)](#file-app-api-reference-client-type-aliases-appguid) - GUID string for identifying app entities (App).

- [AppStartingCallback](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-appstartingcallback) - Callback that receives a `RootAppStartState` (App).

- [AssetClient](#file-app-api-reference-server-interfaces-assetclient) - Client for converting temporary upload tokens into permanent asset URIs.

- [AssetAppCreateResponse](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-assetappcreateresponse) - Response containing the permanent asset URIs created from upload tokens.

- [AssetFile](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-assetfile) - Object type with properties: extensions, length, mimeType, url (Assets).

- [AssetPreview](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-assetpreview) - Object type with properties: description, details, previews, title, ... (Assets).
- [AssetPreviewAudio](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-assetpreviewaudio) - Object type with properties: bitrate, codec, duration, format, ... (Assets).
- [AssetPreviewImage](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-assetpreviewimage) - Object type with properties: height, url, width (Assets).
- [AssetPreviewVideo](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-assetpreviewvideo) - Object type with properties: bitrate, codec, duration, format, ... (Assets).
- [AssetPreviewWebpage](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-assetpreviewwebpage) - Object type with properties: embedUrl, favicon, siteName (Assets).
- [AssetVideo](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-assetvideo) - Object type with properties: aspectRatio, dashUrl, downloadUrl, duration, ... (Assets).

#### Channels

- [ChannelGroupGuid (client)](#file-app-api-reference-client-type-aliases-channelgroupguid) - GUID string for identifying channel group entities (Channels).
- [ChannelGuid (client)](#file-app-api-reference-client-type-aliases-channelguid) - GUID string for identifying channel entities (Channels).
- [ChannelDirectoryEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-enumerations-channeldirectoryevent) - Enum providing string constants for directory event names. Use these values when subscribing to events on `ChannelDirectoryClient`.
- [ChannelEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-enumerations-channelevent) - Enum providing string constants for channel event names. Use these values when subscribing to events on `ChannelClient`.
- [ChannelFileEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-enumerations-channelfileevent) - Enum providing string constants for file event names. Use these values when subscribing to events on `ChannelFileClient`.
- [ChannelGroupEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-enumerations-channelgroupevent) - Enum of event names for channel group changes. Use these values when subscribing to events on `ChannelGroupClient`.
- [ChannelMessageEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-enumerations-channelmessageevent) - Enum providing string constants for message event names. Use these values when subscribing to events on `ChannelMessageClient`.
- [ChannelType](#file-app-api-reference-server-enumerations-channeltype) - Enum defining the types of channels that can be created in a community.
- [ChannelWebRtcEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-enumerations-channelwebrtcevent) - Enum providing string constants for voice channel event names. Use these values when subscribing to events on `ChannelWebRtcClient`.
- [Channel](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channel) - Represents a channel within a community. Channels are the primary containers for content and belong to channel groups.
- [ChannelClient](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelclient) - Service client for managing channels within a community. Channels are the primary containers for content within channel groups, supporting different...
- [ChannelCreatedEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelcreatedevent) - Event data emitted when a channel becomes visible to your code.
- [ChannelCreatedHandler](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelcreatedhandler) - Event handler that receives a `ChannelCreatedEvent` (Channels).
- [ChannelCreateRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelcreaterequest) - Request object for creating a new channel within a channel group.
- [ChannelDeletedEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeldeletedevent) - Event data emitted when a channel is no longer visible to your code.
- [ChannelDeletedHandler](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeldeletedhandler) - Event handler that receives a `ChannelDeletedEvent` (Channels).
- [ChannelDeleteRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeldeleterequest) - Request object for deleting a channel.
- [ChannelDirectory](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeldirectory) - Represents a directory (folder) within a channel's file system.
- [ChannelDirectoryClient](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeldirectoryclient) - Service client for managing directories (folders) within a channel's file system.
- [ChannelDirectoryCreatedEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeldirectorycreatedevent) - Event payload emitted when a directory is created.
- [ChannelDirectoryCreateRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeldirectorycreaterequest) - Request object for creating a new directory in a channel.
- [ChannelDirectoryDeletedEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeldirectorydeletedevent) - Event payload emitted when a directory is deleted.
- [ChannelDirectoryDeleteRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeldirectorydeleterequest) - Request object for deleting a directory and all its contents.
- [ChannelDirectoryEditedEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeldirectoryeditedevent) - Event payload emitted when a directory is renamed.
- [ChannelDirectoryEditRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeldirectoryeditrequest) - Request object for renaming a directory.
- [ChannelDirectoryEditResponse](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeldirectoryeditresponse) - Response object returned after renaming a directory.
- [ChannelDirectoryEvents](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeldirectoryevents) - Event map type for `ChannelDirectoryClient`. This type defines the event signatures for directory-related events.
- [ChannelDirectoryGetRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeldirectorygetrequest) - Request object for retrieving a single directory.
- [ChannelDirectoryListRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeldirectorylistrequest) - Request object for listing all directories in a channel. Returns a flat array containing all directories regardless of nesting depth.
- [ChannelDirectoryMovedEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeldirectorymovedevent) - Event payload emitted when a directory is moved to a different parent.
- [ChannelDirectoryMoveRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeldirectorymoverequest) - Request object for moving a directory to a different parent directory.
- [ChannelDirectoryMoveResponse](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeldirectorymoveresponse) - Response object returned after moving a directory.
- [ChannelEditedEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeleditedevent) - Event data emitted when a visible channel's properties or permissions change.
- [ChannelEditedHandler](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeleditedhandler) - Event handler that receives a `ChannelEditedEvent` (Channels).
- [ChannelEditRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeleditrequest) - Request object for editing an existing channel's properties.
- [ChannelEvents](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelevents) - Event map type for `ChannelClient`. This type defines the event signatures for channel-related events.
- [ChannelFile](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelfile) - Represents a file stored within a channel directory. Files contain metadata about uploaded content and link to assets in the asset system.
- [ChannelFileClient](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelfileclient) - Service client for managing files within channel directories. Files are stored in directories and linked to assets in the asset system.
- [ChannelFileCreatedEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelfilecreatedevent) - Event payload emitted when a file is created.
- [ChannelFileCreateRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelfilecreaterequest) - Request object for creating a new file entry in a directory.
- [ChannelFileDeletedEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelfiledeletedevent) - Event payload emitted when a file is deleted.
- [ChannelFileDeleteRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelfiledeleterequest) - Request object for deleting a file.
- [ChannelFileEditedEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelfileeditedevent) - Event payload emitted when a file is renamed.
- [ChannelFileEditRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelfileeditrequest) - Request object for renaming a file.
- [ChannelFileEditResponse](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelfileeditresponse) - Response object returned after renaming a file.
- [ChannelFileEvents](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelfileevents) - Event map type for `ChannelFileClient`. This type defines the event signatures for file-related events.
- [ChannelFileGetRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelfilegetrequest) - Request object for retrieving a single file.
- [ChannelFileListRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelfilelistrequest) - Request object for listing all files in a directory.
- [ChannelFileMovedEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelfilemovedevent) - Event payload emitted when a file is moved to a different directory.
- [ChannelFileMoveRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelfilemoverequest) - Request object for moving a file to a different directory.
- [ChannelFileMoveResponse](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelfilemoveresponse) - Response object returned after moving a file.
- [ChannelFileSearchCommunityRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelfilesearchcommunityrequest) - Request object for searching files by name across multiple channels.
- [ChannelFileSearchCommunityResponse](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelfilesearchcommunityresponse) - Response object returned from a community-wide file search.
- [ChannelFileSearchRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelfilesearchrequest) - Request object for searching files by name within a single channel.
- [ChannelFileSearchResult](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelfilesearchresult) - Search result for a single channel in a community-wide file search.
- [ChannelGetRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelgetrequest) - Request object for retrieving a single channel by its ID.
- [ChannelGroup](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelgroup) - Represents a container that organizes channels within a community.
- [ChannelGroupClient](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelgroupclient) - Service client for managing channel groups within a community.
- [ChannelGroupCreatedEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelgroupcreatedevent) - Event payload emitted when a channel group becomes visible to your code.
- [ChannelGroupCreatedHandler](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelgroupcreatedhandler) - Event handler that receives a `ChannelGroupCreatedEvent` (Channels).
- [ChannelGroupCreateRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelgroupcreaterequest) - Request to create a new channel group in the community.
- [ChannelGroupDeletedEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelgroupdeletedevent) - Event payload emitted when a channel group is no longer visible to your code.
- [ChannelGroupDeletedHandler](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelgroupdeletedhandler) - Event handler that receives a `ChannelGroupDeletedEvent` (Channels).
- [ChannelGroupDeleteRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelgroupdeleterequest) - Request to delete a channel group. Deleting a channel group also deletes all channels within it.
- [ChannelGroupEditedEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelgroupeditedevent) - Event payload emitted when a visible channel group's properties or permissions change.
- [ChannelGroupEditedHandler](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelgroupeditedhandler) - Event handler that receives a `ChannelGroupEditedEvent` (Channels).
- [ChannelGroupEditRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelgroupeditrequest) - Request to edit an existing channel group.
- [ChannelGroupEvents](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelgroupevents) - Event map type for `ChannelGroupClient`. This type defines the event signatures for channel group events.
- [ChannelGroupGetRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelgroupgetrequest) - Request to retrieve a single channel group.
- [ChannelGroupGuid (server)](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelgroupguid) - GUID string for identifying channel group entities (Channels).
- [ChannelGroupMovedEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelgroupmovedevent) - Event payload emitted when a visible channel group is moved to a different position in the sidebar.
- [ChannelGroupMoveRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelgroupmoverequest) - Request to move a channel group to a different position in the sidebar.
- [ChannelGuid (server)](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelguid) - GUID string for identifying channel entities (Channels).
- [ChannelListRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channellistrequest) - Request object for listing all channels within a channel group.
- [ChannelMessage](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelmessage) - Represents a message in a channel.
- [ChannelMessageClient](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelmessageclient) - Service client for managing messages within channels. Messages are the primary content units in text channels, supporting rich content with...
- [ChannelMessageCreatedEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelmessagecreatedevent) - Event data emitted when a new message is created in a channel.
- [ChannelMessageCreateRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelmessagecreaterequest) - Request object for creating a new message in a channel.
- [ChannelMessageDeletedEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelmessagedeletedevent) - Event data emitted when a message is deleted from a channel.
- [ChannelMessageDeleteRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelmessagedeleterequest) - Request object for deleting a message from a channel.
- [ChannelMessageEditedEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelmessageeditedevent) - Event data emitted when a message's content is modified.
- [ChannelMessageEditRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelmessageeditrequest) - Request object for editing an existing message.
- [ChannelMessageEvents](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelmessageevents) - Event map type for `ChannelMessageClient`. This type defines the event signatures for message-related events.
- [ChannelMessageFlagRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelmessageflagrequest) - Request object for flagging a message for moderation review.
- [ChannelMessageGetRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelmessagegetrequest) - Request object for retrieving a single message by ID.
- [ChannelMessageListRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelmessagelistrequest) - Request object for listing messages in a channel with pagination.
- [ChannelMessageListResponse](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelmessagelistresponse) - Response object returned when listing messages in a channel.
- [ChannelMessagePinCreatedEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelmessagepincreatedevent) - Event data emitted when a message is pinned to a channel.

- [ChannelMessageReactionCreatedEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelmessagereactioncreatedevent) - Event data emitted when a reaction is added to a message.
- [ChannelMessageReactionCreateRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelmessagereactioncreaterequest) - Request object for adding a reaction to a message.
- [ChannelMessageReactionDeletedEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelmessagereactiondeletedevent) - Event data emitted when a reaction is removed from a message.
- [ChannelMessageReactionDeleteRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelmessagereactiondeleterequest) - Request object for removing a reaction from a message.
- [ChannelMessageSetTypingIndicatorEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelmessagesettypingindicatorevent) - Event data emitted when a user's typing indicator status changes in a channel.
- [ChannelMessageSetTypingIndicatorRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelmessagesettypingindicatorrequest) - Request object for updating the typing indicator status in a channel.
- [ChannelMessageSetViewTimeEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelmessagesetviewtimeevent) - Object type with properties: channelId, communityId, userLastViewedAt (Channels).
- [ChannelMessageSetViewTimeRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelmessagesetviewtimerequest) - Request object for updating the last viewed timestamp in a channel.
- [ChannelMovedEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelmovedevent) - Event data emitted when a visible channel is moved to a different position or channel group.
- [ChannelMoveRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelmoverequest) - Request object for moving a channel to a different position or channel group.
- [ChannelOrChannelGroupGuid](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelorchannelgroupguid) - GUID string for identifying channel or channel group entities (Channels).
- [ChannelOverlayPermission](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeloverlaypermission) - Defines permission overrides for channel-level operations. Used in access rules to grant or deny specific permissions to roles or members on...
- [ChannelPermission](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelpermission) - Permissions that control actions on a specific channel or its contents.
- [ChannelWebRtc](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelwebrtc) - Object type with properties: channelId, communityId, deviceId, isAdminDeafened, ... (Channels).
- [ChannelWebRtcClient](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelwebrtcclient) - Service client for monitoring and moderating voice channel participants.
- [ChannelWebRtcEvents](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelwebrtcevents) - Event map type for `ChannelWebRtcClient`. This type defines the event signatures for voice channel-related events.
- [ChannelWebRtcKickRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelwebrtckickrequest) - Request object for removing a participant from a voice channel.
- [ChannelWebRtcListRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelwebrtclistrequest) - Request object for listing participants in a voice channel.
- [ChannelWebRtcListResponse](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelwebrtclistresponse) - Response object returned when listing voice channel participants.
- [ChannelWebRtcSetMuteAndDeafenOtherRequest](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelwebrtcsetmuteanddeafenotherrequest) - Request object for applying server-side mute or deafen state to a voice channel participant.
- [ChannelWebRtcUserAttachEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelwebrtcuserattachevent) - Event payload emitted when a user joins a voice channel.
- [ChannelWebRtcUserDetachEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelwebrtcuserdetachevent) - Event payload emitted when a user leaves a voice channel.
- [ChannelWebRtcUserDeviceSetDataChannelEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelwebrtcuserdevicesetdatachannelevent) - Event payload emitted when a user's data channel configuration changes in a voice channel.
- [ChannelWebRtcUserDeviceSetStatusEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelwebrtcuserdevicesetstatusevent) - Event payload emitted when a user's mute or deafen state changes in a voice channel.
- [ChannelWebRtcUserDeviceSetTransportEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelwebrtcuserdevicesettransportevent) - Event payload emitted when a user enables or disables their microphone, camera, or screen sharing in a voice channel.

#### Client

- [ClientEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-enumerations-clientevent) - Event names for client attachment changes.
- [Client](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-client) - Represents a community member who currently has your app's channel open on one or more devices.
- [ClientContext](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-clientcontext) - Identifies a specific member and device. Used in device-level attachment events (`ClientEvent.UserDeviceAttached`, `ClientEvent.UserDeviceDetached`).
- [ClientEvents](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-clientevents) - Event map type for `AttachedClients`. This type defines the event signatures for client attachment events.

- [CommunityRoleGuid (client)](#file-app-api-reference-client-type-aliases-communityroleguid) - GUID string for identifying community role entities (Community).
- [CommunityAppLogType](#file-app-api-reference-server-enumerations-communityapplogtype) - Enum defining severity levels for community app log entries.

- [CommunityEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-enumerations-communityevent) - Enum providing string constants for community event names. Use these values when subscribing to events on `CommunityClient`.
- [CommunityLeaveReason](#file-app-api-reference-server-enumerations-communityleavereason) - Enum indicating why a user left a community.
- [CommunityMemberBanEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-enumerations-communitymemberbanevent) - Enum providing string constants for community member ban event names. Use these values when subscribing to events on `CommunityMemberBanClient`.

- [CommunityJoinedEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-communityjoinedevent) - Event data emitted when a user joins the community.

- [CommunityLeaveEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-communityleaveevent) - Event data emitted when a user leaves the community.
- [CommunityMember](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-communitymember) - Represents a user's membership in a community, including their profile information, assigned roles, and membership dates.
- [CommunityMemberAttachEvent](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-communitymemberattachevent) - Event payload emitted every time a community member opens the community on any device.
- [CommunityMemberBan](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-communitymemberban) - Represents a ban record for a community member. Bans prevent users from rejoining the community until the ban is deleted or expires.
- [CommunityMemberBanBulk](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-communitymemberbanbulk) - Response object for bulk ban operations, indicating which users were successfully banned.

- [UnknownGuid](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-unknownguid) - GUID string for identifying unknown entities.
- [assetClient](#file-app-api-reference-server-variables-assetclient) - Constant of type `AssetClient`.
- [EmptyGuid (server)](#file-app-api-reference-server-variables-emptyguid) - A constant representing an empty GUID value.
- [rootServer](#file-app-api-reference-server-variables-rootserver) - Constant of type `RootServer`.
- [WellKnownRootGuids](#file-app-api-reference-server-variables-wellknownrootguids) - Constant of type `object`.

---

# App API Reference

TypeScript API reference for the Root App SDK. Server API covers community access, persistence, networking, job scheduling, and lifecycle hooks. Client API covers UI integration, theme support, and client-server communication.

---

## File: app-api-reference/client/classes/RootGuidUtils.md

Utility class for working with Root GUIDs, providing methods to convert between
different formats (UUID, GUID, and string) and to extract metadata like timestamps.

## File: app-api-reference/client/classes/UserRoleSelector.md

A searchable dropdown component for selecting users and community roles. Use this component when you need to let users pick one or more community members or roles, such as for assigning permissions, mentions, or task assignments.

The component fetches available users and roles from the Root SDK and displays them in collapsible, searchable sections. Selected items appear first in each section for easy visibility.

**Installation**

Add to your client's `package.json`:

```json
{
  "dependencies": {
    "@rootsdk/client-app-ui": "^0.1.0",
    "lit": "^3.2.1",
    "lit-element": "^4.1.1"
  }
}
```

The `lit` and `lit-element` packages are peer dependencies required by the component.

**Import**

```typescript
import '@rootsdk/client-app-ui';
```

**Basic usage**

```html
<rootsdk-user-role-selector></rootsdk-user-role-selector>
```

**Events**

The component dispatches a `rootsdk-user-role-selector:selection-change` CustomEvent whenever the selection changes. The event detail contains `{ selectedUserIds: string[], selectedRoleIds: string[] }`.

**Examples**

Multi-select mode (default):

```html
<rootsdk-user-role-selector
  mode="multi"
></rootsdk-user-role-selector>
```

Single-select mode:

```html
<rootsdk-user-role-selector
  mode="single"
></rootsdk-user-role-selector>
```

Pre-selected values:

```html
<rootsdk-user-role-selector
  .selectedUserIds=${['user-123', 'user-456']}
  .selectedRoleIds=${['role-admin']}
></rootsdk-user-role-selector>
```

Handling selection changes:

```typescript
const selector = document.querySelector('rootsdk-user-role-selector');

selector.addEventListener('rootsdk-user-role-selector:selection-change', (event) => {
  const { selectedUserIds, selectedRoleIds } = event.detail;
  console.log('Selected users:', selectedUserIds);
  console.log('Selected roles:', selectedRoleIds);
});
```

Using with Lit:

```typescript
import { LitElement, html } from 'lit';
import { customElement, state } from 'lit/decorators.js';
import '@rootsdk/client-app-ui';

@customElement('my-app')
class MyApp extends LitElement {
  @state() selectedUsers: string[] = [];
  @state() selectedRoles: string[] = [];

  handleSelectionChange(e: CustomEvent) {
    this.selectedUsers = e.detail.selectedUserIds;
    this.selectedRoles = e.detail.selectedRoleIds;
  }

  render() {
    return html`
      <rootsdk-user-role-selector
        mode="multi"
        .selectedUserIds=${this.selectedUsers}
        .selectedRoleIds=${this.selectedRoles}
        @rootsdk-user-role-selector:selection-change=${this.handleSelectionChange}
      ></rootsdk-user-role-selector>
    `;
  }
}
```

Using with React:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import '@rootsdk/client-app-ui';

function UserRoleSelector() {
  const selectorRef = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
  const [selectedUsers, setSelectedUsers] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [selectedRoles, setSelectedRoles] = useState<string[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const selector = selectorRef.current;
    if (!selector) return;

    const handleChange = (e: CustomEvent) => {
      setSelectedUsers(e.detail.selectedUserIds);
      setSelectedRoles(e.detail.selectedRoleIds);
    };

    selector.addEventListener('rootsdk-user-role-selector:selection-change', handleChange);
    return () => {
      selector.removeEventListener('rootsdk-user-role-selector:selection-change', handleChange);
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <rootsdk-user-role-selector
      ref={selectorRef}
      mode="multi"
    />
  );
}
```

To set initial values in React, update the element properties after mounting:

```tsx
useEffect(() => {
  const selector = selectorRef.current;
  if (!selector) return;

  (selector as any).selectedUserIds = ['user-123'];
  (selector as any).selectedRoleIds = ['role-admin'];
}, []);
```

## Extends

- `LitElement`

## Constructors

### Constructor

> **new UserRoleSelector**(): `UserRoleSelector`

#### Returns

`LitElement.constructor`

## Properties

### mode

> **mode**: `"single"` | `"multi"` = `"multi"`

The selection mode. Use `"multi"` (default) to allow selecting multiple users and roles simultaneously. Use `"single"` to allow only one selection at a time—selecting a user clears any selected role and vice versa.

### selectedRoleIds

> **selectedRoleIds**: `string`[] = `[]`

Array of community role IDs that should appear as selected. Pass this prop to control the component's selection state.

### selectedUserIds

> **selectedUserIds**: `string`[] = `[]`

Array of user IDs that should appear as selected. Pass this prop to control the component's selection state.

### view

> **view**: `"both"` | `"roles"` | `"members"` = `"both"`

### styles

> `static` **styles**: `CSSResult`[]

The component's styles defined using Lit's CSS tagged template literal. These styles are scoped to the component using Shadow DOM.

#### Overrides

`LitElement.styles`

## Methods

### connectedCallback()

#### Returns

#### Returns

`TemplateResult`<`1`>

#### Overrides

`LitElement.render`

## File: app-api-reference/client/enumerations/RootServerExceptionType.md

Indicates the type of problem that occurred during a remote call from an App's client to its server. This type is used in cases where the Root server-side infrastructure detects the failure. The Root server-side infrastructure will load one of these values into a `RootServerException` and send the exception across to an App's client.

## Enumeration Members

### MessageTooLong

> **MessageTooLong**: `-7`

Indicates either the client's message to the server or the server's response exceeded the 512K limit.

### RateLimitExceeded

> **RateLimitExceeded**: `-8`

### RequestTimeout

> **RequestTimeout**: `-2`

Indicates the server took too long to service the client's request. The exception does not include any information about how much of the server-side work completed before the timeout occurred. The request may have completed before the timeout; if you choose to resend the same request from your client, be sure your server can tolerate receiving a duplicate request. Alternatively, you could notify the user of the issue and restart your client.

### RootInternalException

> **RootInternalException**: `-4`

Indicates an issue occurred inside the Root client or server infrastructure. The problem is not likely in your code. Consider restarting your client.

### UnhandledException

> **UnhandledException**: `-3`

Indicates an unhandled exception other than `RootApiException` occurred on the server. Typically, this means your server code generated an exception that you didn't catch. The Root infrastructure caught the exception, translated it into a `RootServerException` with the code set to `UnhandledException`, and sent that `RootServerException` across to your client. Your immediate response will likely be to restart your client. Longer term, you'll likely want to update your server code so it catches these exceptions.

### UnhandledRootApiException

> **UnhandledRootApiException**: `-1`

Indicates a `RootApiException` was unhandled on the server. The Root infrastructure on the server translated the unhandled `RootApiException` into a `RootServerException` and set the code inside the `RootServerException` to `UnhandledRootApiException`. Your immediate response will likely be to restart your client. You may want to update your server code so it catches these exceptions.

### Unspecified

> **Unspecified**: `0`

This is the default code used by `RootServerException`. If you see it in your client, it typically means the server-side code that threw the exception did not explicitly set a value for the code.

---

## File: app-api-reference/server/classes/RootApiException.md

Represents an error generated by the Root API. This error includes additional information such as error codes, payloads, and metadata.

## Extends

- `Error`

## Properties

### errorCode

> **errorCode**: [`ErrorCodeType`](#file-app-api-reference-server-enumerations-errorcodetype) = `ErrorCodeType.Unspecified`

The specific error code related to the error.

### id?

> `optional` **id**: [`RootGuid`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-rootguid)

Optional unique identifier for this error instance.

### parentId

> **parentId**: [`RootGuid`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-rootguid) | `undefined`

Optional identifier for the parent error, if this error is part of a chain.

### payload?

> `optional` **payload**: `RootGrpcExceptionPayload`

The specific payload associated with this error, which can be one of several predefined exception payload types.

### whatId

> **whatId**: [`RootGuid`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-rootguid) | `undefined`

Identifier for what caused the error.

### whereId

> **whereId**: [`RootGuid`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-rootguid) | `undefined`

Identifier for where the error occurred.

### whoId?

> `optional` **whoId**: [`UserGuid`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-userguid)

Optional identifier for the entity responsible for the error.

---

## File: app-api-reference/server/enumerations/ChannelDirectoryEvent.md

Enum providing string constants for directory event names. Use these values when subscribing to events on `ChannelDirectoryClient`.

## Enumeration Members

### ChannelDirectoryCreated

> **ChannelDirectoryCreated**: `"channelDirectory.created"`

Emitted when a directory is created.

### ChannelDirectoryDeleted

> **ChannelDirectoryDeleted**: `"channelDirectory.deleted"`

Emitted when a directory is deleted.

### ChannelDirectoryEdited

> **ChannelDirectoryEdited**: `"channelDirectory.edited"`

Emitted when a directory is renamed.

### ChannelDirectoryMoved

> **ChannelDirectoryMoved**: `"channelDirectory.moved"`

Emitted when a directory is moved to a different parent.

---

## File: app-api-reference/server/enumerations/ChannelEvent.md

Enum providing string constants for channel event names. Use these values when subscribing to events on `ChannelClient`.

## Enumeration Members

### ChannelCreated

> **ChannelCreated**: `"channel.created"`

Emitted when a channel becomes visible to your code. This includes newly created channels and existing channels that your code can now see due to permission changes.

### ChannelDeleted

> **ChannelDeleted**: `"channel.deleted"`

Emitted when a channel is no longer visible to your code. This includes deleted channels and existing channels that your code can no longer see due to permission changes.

### ChannelEdited

> **ChannelEdited**: `"channel.edited"`

Emitted when a visible channel's properties or permissions change.

### ChannelMoved

> **ChannelMoved**: `"channel.moved"`

Emitted when a visible channel is moved to a different position or channel group.

---

## File: app-api-reference/server/enumerations/ChannelFileEvent.md

### ChannelFileCreated

> **ChannelFileCreated**: `"channelFile.created"`

Emitted when a file is created.

### ChannelFileDeleted

> **ChannelFileDeleted**: `"channelFile.deleted"`

Emitted when a file is deleted.

### ChannelFileEdited

> **ChannelFileEdited**: `"channelFile.edited"`

Emitted when a file is renamed.

### ChannelFileMoved

> **ChannelFileMoved**: `"channelFile.moved"`

Emitted when a file is moved to a different directory.

## File: app-api-reference/server/enumerations/ChannelGroupEvent.md

Enum of event names for channel group changes. Use these values when subscribing to events on `ChannelGroupClient`.

## Enumeration Members

### ChannelGroupCreated

> **ChannelGroupCreated**: `"channelGroup.created"`

Fired when a channel group becomes visible to your code. This includes newly created channel groups and existing channel groups that your code can now see due to permission changes.

### ChannelGroupDeleted

> **ChannelGroupDeleted**: `"channelGroup.deleted"`

Fired when a channel group is no longer visible to your code. This includes deleted channel groups and existing channel groups that your code can no longer see due to permission changes.

### ChannelGroupEdited

> **ChannelGroupEdited**: `"channelGroup.edited"`

Fired when a visible channel group's properties or permissions change.

### ChannelGroupMoved

> **ChannelGroupMoved**: `"channelGroup.moved"`

Fired when a visible channel group is moved to a different position.

---

## File: app-api-reference/server/enumerations/ClientEvent.md

Event names for client attachment changes.

## Enumeration Members

### UserAttached

> **UserAttached**: `"user.attached"`

Emitted when a member's first device opens your app's channel. Use this to detect when a member becomes present. The callback receives a `Client` object.

### UserDetached

> **UserDetached**: `"user.detached"`

Emitted when a member's last device navigates away from your app's channel. Use this to detect when a member is no longer present. The callback receives a `Client` object.

### UserDeviceAttached

> **UserDeviceAttached**: `"user.device.attached"`

Emitted when an additional device attaches for a member who is already present. The member was already attached via another device. The callback receives a `ClientContext` object.

### UserDeviceDetached

> **UserDeviceDetached**: `"user.device.detached"`

Emitted when one device detaches but the member remains attached via other devices. The callback receives a `ClientContext` object.

## File: app-api-reference/server/enumerations/CommunityMemberEvent.md

Enum providing string constants for community member event names. Use these values when subscribing to events on `CommunityMemberClient`.

## Enumeration Members

### CommunityMemberAttach

> **CommunityMemberAttach**: `"communityMember.attach"`

Emitted when a community member becomes present in the community on a device. This happens when:

- **Desktop client:** The user opens the community in a tab (including popout windows). All open tabs are attached simultaneously — switching between tabs does not trigger additional attach/detach events.
- **Mobile client:** The user opens the community (only one community is open at a time).
- **Apps:** The app's host process connects to the community during deployment or dynamic assignment.
- **Reconnection:** When a client reconnects after a connection loss, it re-attaches to all previously open communities.

The event fires each time a device attaches, even if the user is already attached on another device.

### CommunityMemberDetach

> **CommunityMemberDetach**: `"communityMember.detach"`

Emitted when a community member is no longer present in the community on any device. This happens when:

- **Desktop client:** The user closes the community's tab or popout window. Switching away from a tab does not trigger detach.
- **Mobile client:** The user navigates away from the community.
- **Connection loss:** The user's connection drops and they have no remaining devices attached to the community.
- **Leaving or removal:** The user leaves the community, or is kicked/banned.
- **Apps:** The app's host process disconnects or the community is unassigned.

The event is only broadcast once the user has no remaining attached devices in the community — if they have the same community open on two devices and close one, no detach is emitted.

### UserSetProfile

> **UserSetProfile**: `"user.set.profile"`

Emitted when a user updates their username or profile picture.

---

## File: app-api-reference/server/enumerations/JobScheduleEvent.md

Enum providing string constants for job notification event names. Use these values when subscribing to events on `JobScheduler`.

## Enumeration Members

### Job

> **Job**: `"job"`

Raised at a job's regularly-scheduled time.

#### Example

```ts
import
{
  jobScheduler,
  JobData,
  JobScheduleEvent,
}
from '@rootsdk/server-app'

function subscribe()
{
  jobScheduler.addListener(JobScheduleEvent.Job, handleJobEvent);
}

const handleJobEvent = (event: JobData) =>
{
  const jobScheduleId: string = event.jobScheduleId;
  const resourceId:    string = event.resourceId;
  const tag:           string = event.tag || 'No tag';
  const jobTime:       Date   = event.jobTime;

  //...
};
```

### JobMissed

> **JobMissed**: `"job.missed"`

Raised if your server wasn't available at the regularly-scheduled event time.

#### Example

```ts
import
{
  jobScheduler,
  JobData,
  JobScheduleEvent,
}
from '@rootsdk/server-app'

function subscribe()
{
  jobScheduler.addListener(JobScheduleEvent.JobMissed, handleJobMissedEvent);
}

const handleJobMissedEvent = (event: JobData) =>
{
  const jobScheduleId: string = event.jobScheduleId;
  const resourceId:    string = event.resourceId;
  const tag:           string = event.tag || 'No tag';
  const jobTime:       Date   = event.jobTime;

  // ...
};
```

## File: app-api-reference/server/enumerations/RootServerExceptionType.md

Indicates the type of problem that occurred during a remote call from an App's client to its server. This type is used in cases where the Root server-side infrastructure detects the failure. The Root server-side infrastructure will load one of these values into a `RootServerException` and send the exception across to an App's client.

## Enumeration Members

### MessageTooLong

> **MessageTooLong**: `-7`

Indicates either the client's message to the server or the server's response exceeded the 512K limit.

### RateLimitExceeded

> **RateLimitExceeded**: `-8`

### RequestTimeout

> **RequestTimeout**: `-2`

Indicates the server took too long to service the client's request. The exception does not include any information about how much of the server-side work completed before the timeout occurred. The request may have completed before the timeout; if you choose to resend the same request from your client, be sure your server can tolerate receiving a duplicate request. Alternatively, you could notify the user of the issue and restart your client.

### RootInternalException

> **RootInternalException**: `-4`

Indicates an issue occurred inside the Root client or server infrastructure. The problem is not likely in your code. Consider restarting your client.

### UnhandledException

> **UnhandledException**: `-3`

Indicates an unhandled exception other than `RootApiException` occurred on the server. Typically, this means your server code generated an exception that you didn't catch. The Root infrastructure caught the exception, translated it into a `RootServerException` with the code set to `UnhandledException`, and sent that `RootServerException` across to your client. Your immediate response will likely be to restart your client. Longer term, you'll likely want to update your server code so it catches these exceptions.

### UnhandledRootApiException

> **UnhandledRootApiException**: `-1`

Indicates a `RootApiException` was unhandled on the server. The Root infrastructure on the server translated the unhandled `RootApiException` into a `RootServerException` and set the code inside the `RootServerException` to `UnhandledRootApiException`. Your immediate response will likely be to restart your client. You may want to update your server code so it catches these exceptions.

### Unspecified

> **Unspecified**: `0`

This is the default code used by `RootServerException`. If you see it in your client, it typically means the server-side code that threw the exception did not explicitly set a value for the code.

---

## File: app-api-reference/server/interfaces/AssetClient.md

Client for converting temporary upload tokens into permanent asset URIs. Use this to persist files that your app's client has uploaded so they can be referenced later.

This client is only available to apps. Bots cannot create assets.

Access this client via `rootServer.dataStore.assets`.

## Methods

### create()

> **create**(`request`: [`AssetAppCreateRequest`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-assetappcreaterequest)): `Promise`<[`AssetAppCreateResponse`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-assetappcreateresponse)>

Converts one or more upload tokens into permanent asset URIs. Upload tokens come from your app's client code when users select files through the file picker. Once converted, the resulting asset URIs can be stored and used indefinitely.

#### Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| ------ | ------ | ------ |
| `request` | [`AssetAppCreateRequest`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-assetappcreaterequest) | The upload tokens to convert. |

#### Returns

`Promise`<[`AssetAppCreateResponse`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-assetappcreateresponse)>

A promise that resolves to an `AssetAppCreateResponse` containing a map of tokens to their permanent asset URIs.

#### Example

```ts
import {
  AssetAppCreateRequest,
  AssetAppCreateResponse,
  rootServer,
} from "@rootsdk/server-app";

export async function createExample(
  tokens: string[],
): Promise<AssetAppCreateResponse> {
  try {
    // Set up the request
    const request: AssetAppCreateRequest = {
      tokens: tokens,
    };

    // Call the API
    const result: AssetAppCreateResponse =
      await rootServer.dataStore.assets.create(request);

    return result;
  } catch (error) {
    // Detect error
    throw error;
  }
}
```

### get()

> **get**(`request`: `AssetGetRequest`): `Promise`<`AssetGetResponse`>

#### Parameters

| Parameter | Type |
| ------ | ------ |
| `request` | `AssetGetRequest` |

#### Returns

`Promise`<`AssetGetResponse`>

## File: app-api-reference/server/type-aliases/AccessRule.md

> **AccessRule** = `object`

Represents a permission override for a specific role or member on a specific channel or channel group.

Access rules enable fine-grained permission control by allowing or denying specific channel permissions for individual roles or members, independent of their default role permissions.

## Properties

### channelOrChannelGroupId

> **channelOrChannelGroupId**: [`ChannelOrChannelGroupGuid`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelorchannelgroupguid)

The `ChannelOrChannelGroupUuid` identifying the channel or channel group this access rule applies to.

### overlay

> **overlay**: [`ChannelOverlayPermission`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeloverlaypermission)

The `ChannelOverlayPermission` object containing the permission overrides. Each permission can be set to `true` (explicitly allow), `false` (explicitly deny), or left undefined (do not modify).

### roleOrMemberId

> **roleOrMemberId**: [`RoleOrMemberGuid`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-roleormemberguid)

The `RoleOrMemberUuid` identifying the role or member this access rule applies to.

---

## File: app-api-reference/server/type-aliases/AccessRuleClient.md

> **AccessRuleClient** = `object`

Service client for managing access rules that control permissions for specific roles or members on channels and channel groups.

Access rules allow fine-grained permission control by overriding the default permissions for specific roles or members on specific channels or channel groups.

Access this client via `rootServer.community.accessRules`.

## Methods

### create()

> **create**(`request`: [`AccessRuleCreateRequest`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-accessrulecreaterequest), `eventHandlers?`: `object`): `Promise`<`void`>

Creates a new access rule for a role or member on a channel or channel group.

#### Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| ------ | ------ | ------ |
| `request` | [`AccessRuleCreateRequest`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-accessrulecreaterequest) | The access rule configuration including target, subject, and permission overlay. |
| `eventHandlers?` | \{ `channel.created`: [`ChannelCreatedHandler`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelcreatedhandler); `channel.deleted`: [`ChannelDeletedHandler`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeldeletedhandler); `channel.edited`: [`ChannelEditedHandler`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeleditedhandler); `channelGroup.created`: [`ChannelGroupCreatedHandler`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelgroupcreatedhandler); `channelGroup.deleted`: [`ChannelGroupDeletedHandler`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelgroupdeletedhandler); `channelGroup.edited`: [`ChannelGroupEditedHandler`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelgroupeditedhandler); `community.permission.edited`: [`CommunityPermissionEditedHandler`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-communitypermissioneditedhandler); \} | Optional handlers for permission update events. Supported event keys: `channel.created`, `channel.edited`, `channel.deleted`, `channelGroup.created`, `channelGroup.edited`, `channelGroup.deleted`, `community.permission.edited`. |
| `eventHandlers.channel.created?` | [`ChannelCreatedHandler`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelcreatedhandler) | - |
| `eventHandlers.channel.deleted?` | [`ChannelDeletedHandler`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeldeletedhandler) | - |
| `eventHandlers.channel.edited?` | [`ChannelEditedHandler`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeleditedhandler) | - |
| `eventHandlers.channelGroup.created?` | [`ChannelGroupCreatedHandler`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelgroupcreatedhandler) | - |
| `eventHandlers.channelGroup.deleted?` | [`ChannelGroupDeletedHandler`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelgroupdeletedhandler) | - |
| `eventHandlers.channelGroup.edited?` | [`ChannelGroupEditedHandler`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelgroupeditedhandler) | - |
| `eventHandlers.community.permission.edited?` | [`CommunityPermissionEditedHandler`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-communitypermissioneditedhandler) | - |

#### Returns

`Promise`<`void`>

A promise that resolves when the access rule is created.

#### Throws

`RootApiException` with `errorCode` set to `NoPermissionToCreate` if missing required permissions, `AlreadyExists` if the rule already exists, or `RequestValidationFailed` if the request is invalid.

#### Example

```ts
import {
  ChannelOrChannelGroupGuid,
  RoleOrMemberGuid,
  AccessRuleCreateRequest,
  ChannelOverlayPermission,
  rootServer,
} from "@rootsdk/server-app";

export async function createExample(
  channelOrChannelGroupId: ChannelOrChannelGroupGuid,
  roleOrMemberId: RoleOrMemberGuid,
): Promise<void> {
  try {
    // Set up the request
    // 'undefined' values will not modify existing permissions, you can omit them if desired, they're included here for clarity
    const request: AccessRuleCreateRequest = {
      channelOrChannelGroupId: channelOrChannelGroupId,
      roleOrMemberId: roleOrMemberId,
      overlay: {
        channelFullControl: undefined,
        channelView: true,
        channelUseExternalEmoji: undefined,
        channelCreateMessage: true,
        channelDeleteMessageOther: undefined,
        channelManagePinnedMessages: undefined,
        channelViewMessageHistory: undefined,
        channelCreateMessageAttachment: undefined,
        channelCreateMessageMention: undefined,
        channelCreateMessageReaction: undefined,
        channelMakeMessagePublic: undefined,
        channelMoveUserOther: undefined,
        channelVoiceTalk: undefined,
        channelVoiceMuteOther: undefined,
        channelVoiceDeafenOther: undefined,
        channelVoiceKick: undefined,
        channelVideoStreamMedia: undefined,
        channelCreateFile: undefined,
        channelManageFiles: undefined,
        channelViewFile: undefined,
        channelAppKick: undefined,
      },
    };

    // Call the API
    await rootServer.community.accessRules.create(request);
  } catch (error) {
    // Detect error
    throw error;
  }
}
```

#### Authorization

Declare the following permissions in your manifest:

```json
"permissions": {
  "channel": {
    "fullControl": true
  }
}
```

The community must also create channel access rules that give your code any needed visibility but don't deny these permissions via an overlay:

- `fullControl` on the target channel or channel group

### get()

> **get**(`request`: [`AccessRuleGetRequest`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-accessrulegetrequest)): `Promise`<[`AccessRule`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-accessrule)>

Retrieves a single access rule.

#### Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| ------ | ------ | ------ |
| `request` | [`AccessRuleGetRequest`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-accessrulegetrequest) | Identifies the access rule to retrieve. |

#### Returns

`Promise`<[`AccessRule`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-accessrule)>

A promise that resolves to the `AccessRule` object.

#### Throws

`RootApiException` with `errorCode` set to `NotFound` if the access rule does not exist, or `NoPermissionToRead` if missing required permissions.

#### Example

```ts
import {
  ChannelOrChannelGroupGuid,
  RoleOrMemberGuid,
  AccessRule,
  AccessRuleGetRequest,
  rootServer,
} from "@rootsdk/server-app";

export async function getExample(
  channelOrChannelGroupId: ChannelOrChannelGroupGuid,
  roleOrMemberId: RoleOrMemberGuid,
): Promise<AccessRule> {
  try {
    // Set up the request
    const request: AccessRuleGetRequest = {
      channelOrChannelGroupId: channelOrChannelGroupId,
      roleOrMemberId: roleOrMemberId,
    };

    // Call the API
    const accessRule: AccessRule =
      await rootServer.community.accessRules.get(request);

    return accessRule;
  } catch (error) {
    // Detect error
    throw error;
  }
}
```

### listByChannelOrChannelGroup()

### update()

> **update**(`request`: [`AccessRuleUpdateRequest`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-accessruleupdaterequest), `eventHandlers?`: `object`): `Promise`<`void`>

Performs batch create, edit, and delete operations on access rules.

#### Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Description |
| ------ | ------ | ------ |
| `request` | [`AccessRuleUpdateRequest`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-accessruleupdaterequest) | Contains arrays of access rules to create, edit, and delete. |
| `eventHandlers?` | \{ `channel.created`: [`ChannelCreatedHandler`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelcreatedhandler); `channel.deleted`: [`ChannelDeletedHandler`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeldeletedhandler); `channel.edited`: [`ChannelEditedHandler`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeleditedhandler); `channelGroup.created`: [`ChannelGroupCreatedHandler`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelgroupcreatedhandler); `channelGroup.deleted`: [`ChannelGroupDeletedHandler`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelgroupdeletedhandler); `channelGroup.edited`: [`ChannelGroupEditedHandler`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelgroupeditedhandler); `community.permission.edited`: [`CommunityPermissionEditedHandler`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-communitypermissioneditedhandler); \} | Optional handlers for permission update events. Supported event keys: `channel.created`, `channel.edited`, `channel.deleted`, `channelGroup.created`, `channelGroup.edited`, `channelGroup.deleted`, `community.permission.edited`. |
| `eventHandlers.channel.created?` | [`ChannelCreatedHandler`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelcreatedhandler) | - |
| `eventHandlers.channel.deleted?` | [`ChannelDeletedHandler`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeldeletedhandler) | - |
| `eventHandlers.channel.edited?` | [`ChannelEditedHandler`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeleditedhandler) | - |
| `eventHandlers.channelGroup.created?` | [`ChannelGroupCreatedHandler`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelgroupcreatedhandler) | - |
| `eventHandlers.channelGroup.deleted?` | [`ChannelGroupDeletedHandler`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelgroupdeletedhandler) | - |
| `eventHandlers.channelGroup.edited?` | [`ChannelGroupEditedHandler`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelgroupeditedhandler) | - |
| `eventHandlers.community.permission.edited?` | [`CommunityPermissionEditedHandler`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-communitypermissioneditedhandler) | - |

#### Returns

`Promise`<`void`>

A promise that resolves when all operations complete.

#### Throws

`RootApiException` with `errorCode` indicating the first failed operation.

#### Example

```ts
import {
  ChannelOrChannelGroupGuid,
  RoleOrMemberGuid,
  AccessRuleUpdateRequest,
  AccessRuleCreateRequest,
  AccessRuleEditRequest,
  AccessRuleDeleteRequest,
  ChannelOverlayPermission,
  rootServer,
} from "@rootsdk/server-app";

export async function updateExample(
  channelOrChannelGroupId: ChannelOrChannelGroupGuid,
  roleOrMemberId: RoleOrMemberGuid,
): Promise<void> {
  try {
    // Set up the request
    // 'undefined' values will not modify existing permissions, you can omit them if desired, they're included here for clarity
    const request: AccessRuleUpdateRequest = {
      creates: [
        {
          channelOrChannelGroupId: channelOrChannelGroupId,
          roleOrMemberId: roleOrMemberId,
          overlay: {
            channelFullControl: undefined,
            channelView: true,
            channelUseExternalEmoji: undefined,
            channelCreateMessage: true,
            channelDeleteMessageOther: undefined,
            channelManagePinnedMessages: undefined,
            channelViewMessageHistory: undefined,
            channelCreateMessageAttachment: undefined,
            channelCreateMessageMention: undefined,
            channelCreateMessageReaction: undefined,
            channelMakeMessagePublic: undefined,
            channelMoveUserOther: undefined,
            channelVoiceTalk: undefined,
            channelVoiceMuteOther: undefined,
            channelVoiceDeafenOther: undefined,
            channelVoiceKick: undefined,
            channelVideoStreamMedia: undefined,
            channelCreateFile: undefined,
            channelManageFiles: undefined,
            channelViewFile: undefined,
            channelAppKick: undefined,
          },
        },
      ],
      edits: [],
      deletes: [],
    };

    // Call the API
    await rootServer.community.accessRules.update(request);
  } catch (error) {
    // Detect error
    throw error;
  }
}
```

#### Authorization

Declare the following permissions in your manifest:

```json
"permissions": {
  "channel": {
    "fullControl": true
  }
}
```

The community must also create channel access rules that give your code any needed visibility but don't deny these permissions via an overlay:

- `fullControl` on the target channel or channel group

---

## File: app-api-reference/server/type-aliases/AccessRuleCreateRequest.md

> **AccessRuleCreateRequest** = `object`

Request object for creating a new access rule that overrides permissions for a role or member on a channel or channel group.

## Properties

### channelOrChannelGroupId

> **channelOrChannelGroupId**: [`ChannelOrChannelGroupGuid`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channelorchannelgroupguid)

The `ChannelOrChannelGroupUuid` identifying the channel or channel group to apply the access rule to. Required.

### overlay?

> `optional` **overlay**: [`ChannelOverlayPermission`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-channeloverlaypermission)

Optional `ChannelOverlayPermission` object specifying the permission overrides. Each property can be set to `true` (explicitly allow), `false` (explicitly deny), or left undefined (inherit from role). If not provided, creates an access rule with no explicit overrides.

### roleOrMemberId

> **roleOrMemberId**: [`RoleOrMemberGuid`](#file-app-api-reference-server-type-aliases-roleormemberguid)

The `RoleOrMemberUuid` identifying the role or member to create the access rule for. Required.
